# 2012 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** Android camera roll · IPO
**Star:** IG Android · `itt12-ig-android` · `sites/instagram/android.html`
**ILS June:** 697,089,489 (+101%) · 2,518,453,530
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 314
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt12-ig-android`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2012/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2012/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2012 — 697,089,489 · bans
2. Instagram Android — filter → share
3. Facebook IPO — $38 · Nasdaq delay
4. SOPA blackout — 18 Jan
5. iPhone Maps flop — iOS 6
6. Pinterest — pin ≥2

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt12-ig-android`

**Room:** `years/2012/sites/instagram/android.html`
**Hook:** `data-ig12-share`
**Nostalgia:** IG Android — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty · Vine-as-2012 gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt12-ig-android`.

**Complete writes**
- Filter → share from Android. Writes itt12-ig-android.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2012` · key `itt12-ig-android`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-ig-android` — do not write `itt11-*` or `itt13-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Pinterest → `years/2012/sites/pinterest/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

### L-0001 · `itt12-trail-q` · pages/about.html

**Open:** `/years/2012/pages/about.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="trail-q"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “pages leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `youtube`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="trail-q"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt12-trail-q']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt12-ig-android` stays empty. `itt11-trail-q` and `itt13-trail-q` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “pages leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="trail-q"]`.
- `localStorage['itt12-trail-q']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2012", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt12-trail-q`.
- Does **not** write `itt12-ig-android`.
- **Next:** Next leftover → `/years/2012/sites/amazon/index.html`
